# TC -【Fitness CRM】- [A-01-01-c][契約操作]

**Scope (Unit 4 of the [test plan](../test-plan.md))** — the **契約操作** tab of the member detail screen (`/members/{memberId}?tab=contracts`): the cards **主契約**, **個別会費調整**, **オプション契約**, **キャンペーン適用**, **1DayPass購入履歴** (left column), **契約サマリー** and the **休会・退会履歴** strip (right column), and the five sheets the tab opens — **主契約変更**, **個別会費調整を追加**, **オプション追加**, **オプション変更**, **オプション解約**. Includes the role gates (主契約変更 HQ/System; 調整を追加 HQ/System/Manager; オプション操作 HQ/System/Manager/Staff), the status / unpaid blocks with their stated reasons, the always-on 代理申請 block on the four application sheets, the server-side rules, persistence, the audit record each write leaves, and 1024px layout stress.

**Revision 2026-11-19 — ladyroom slice (`[Ladyroom]`, TC-194 – TC-217):** the candidates of 主契約変更, オプション追加 and オプション変更 are gated by the member's 性別 against the 性別の条件 of the item's 利用できるエリア (and a 主契約's 性別制限) — E-02 FR-016 / FR-008, G-01 FR-M002, G-02 FR-002; tabs Amendment T13–T16, FR-017a. Areas, QA 主契約 and QA オプション are fixtures of the 029 cross-feature run (their own screens are out of scope here).

**Out of scope for this document** — the head-up card, banners and header menus (Units 1–3; this doc only cross-checks the head-up 「個別会費調整中」 badge and the 主契約 badge after a write); the 変更履歴 table itself (Unit 10 — this doc only checks that each write left a record); the A-03 application list the strip links to (link intent only); G-01 / G-02 / G-03 master screens (pickers read them; masters are preconditions); 主契約一括変更 (`A-01 FR-020`); 1DayPass purchase creation; `?sheet=` deep links; cancelling a pending plan change (withdrawn 2026-09-09 — only its **absence** is asserted).

## Priority definitions

| Priority | Meaning                                                                               |
| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| High     | Core/main-flow function cases, and UI issues that are immediately visible to the user |
| Medium   | Validation cases                                                                      |
| Low      | Rare/edge cases                                                                       |

## Common preconditions

> **Credentials (phase 2).** Real Cognito accounts, password `Password@123`, from `src/lib/auth/demo-users.ts` (Headquarter: `lodu4289+headquater@gmail.com`). Sign in through the app or the header 「デモ: ユーザー切替」 (rendered only while `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=development`). Fallback: inject the `SESSION` cookie (`{access_token, token_type: 'Bearer'}`, TTL ~1 h). Run the six-role sweep once, in PC order — never re-type a password (a real account locks for ~15 min).

| ID   | Precondition                                                                                                                 |
| ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PC-A | Signed in as **Headquarter** (`lodu4289+headquater@gmail.com`).                                                              |
| PC-B | Signed in as **System** (`lodu4289+1@gmail.com`).                                                                            |
| PC-C | Signed in as **Manager** (`lodu4289+manager@gmail.com`); target member inside the managed stores.                            |
| PC-D | Signed in as **Staff** (`lodu4289+staff@gmail.com`); target member in the Staff's store unless the case says otherwise.      |
| PC-E | Signed in as **Observer** (`lodu4289+observer@gmail.com`); target member in the Observer's store.                            |
| PC-F | Signed in as **Trainer** (`lodu4289+trainer@gmail.com`) — no access to the screen.                                           |
| PC-G | PC-A, member **F-ACT** open on `?tab=contracts`; `GET /admin/members/{id}` and the tab's reads snapshotted before any write. |
| PC-H | PC-A, member **F-PLAN** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                            |
| PC-I | Member **F-ADJ** open on `?tab=contracts` (role as stated).                                                                  |
| PC-J | Member **F-ADJ-NEW** open on `?tab=contracts` (role as stated).                                                              |
| PC-K | PC-A, member **F-SUSP** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                            |
| PC-L | PC-A, member **F-WDR** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                             |
| PC-M | PC-A, member **F-UNPAID** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                          |
| PC-N | PC-A, member **F-ACT** open with **F-THROW-OPT** (OPT-1, OPT-2) created and visible in オプション契約.                       |
| PC-O | PC-A, member **F-CAMP** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                            |
| PC-P | PC-A, member **F-1DAY** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                            |
| PC-Q | Viewport **1024×768** (the supported floor); below 1024px is out of scope.                                                   |
| PC-R | PC-A with the **same** member open in two browser tabs, both on `?tab=contracts`.                                            |
| PC-S | PC-A, member **F-EMPTY** open on `?tab=contracts`.                                                                           |
| PC-T | Ladyroom fixtures (029 §8) exist: areas (E-02-G), QA 主契約 (023), QA オプション (026), all linked to the fixture store.     |
| PC-U | PC-A, pre-existing member LR-x (named in the case) on `?tab=contracts`; read-only — every sheet left with 「キャンセル」.    |

**Data policy (test-plan §8).** Prefix every typed free text (事由, 解約理由, memos) with `QA-MD-<yyyymmdd>`. The writes of this unit **cannot be undone from the CRM** — **ask the user before each one, one attempt only, and list it in the last section with ids:** 主契約変更 (TC-031, TC-035 — books a job for next month's 1st, no cancel); 個別会費調整 (TC-067, and TC-065 only if agreed — no delete in the UI; the API has `DELETE …/fee-adjustments/{id}` with a required `cancellationReason`, usable for cleanup only with the user's go-ahead); オプション解約 (TC-118, TC-119, TC-122 — **only** on the F-THROW-OPT contracts, never on a pre-existing option); the server-bypass probes TC-083, TC-089 (if the server wrongly accepts, cancel the created option at once with 即時解約). Validation cases type boundary values but **submit only the rejected value** — the accepted values are not submitted. **Date sensitivity:** 翌月1日, 「あと{n}日」 ≤ 14 days, 合意日時 ≤ 60 days, today / next-month-1st start dates, the current month of every picker and the strip — all computed from the real date in JST at run time.

**Ladyroom slice (TC-194 – TC-217; test-plan §8, 029 §8).** Short names: AREA-F / AREA-M / AREA-N = `QA-E02-AREA-F` (女性のみ) / `-M` (男性のみ) / `-N` (なし); PLAN-F / PLAN-M / PLAN-N / PLAN-NOBODY = `QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-…` (NOBODY carries a 女性のみ and a 男性のみ area); OPT-F / OPT-M / OPT-N = `QA-E02-AREA-OPT-…`; PLAN-SEX-F = `QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-SEX-F` (TC-197 only, see there). Before the first case do the 029 §7e pre-flight (AREA-BE, AREA-MOCK) and record each test member's 性別. Pre-existing members (LR-F … LR-U) only open the three sheets and read the candidates — always 「キャンセル」. Every submit (TC-210 – TC-215) runs on a throw-away member (LR-TF, LR-TM) under the ask-first rule above, one attempt each, listed in the last section; an accepted mismatch is cancelled at once where the CRM can (options) and listed where it cannot (plan change). Switch the QA オプション to 有効 only for the option cases and back to 無効 after (026 policy). Never edit an area from this run — the 性別の条件 flip of TC-209 / TC-217 is made and restored by 029 step 13. **Order** (test-plan §9 step 13; 029 §9 step 10): pre-flight → read-only candidate cases (TC-194 – TC-208, TC-216) → replays (TC-210 – TC-212) → happy paths (TC-213 – TC-215) → at 029 step 13: TC-209, TC-217.

## Execution summary

Run 2026-09-30 (JST) by Claude (execute-testcases). App `http://localhost:3000`, backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com` (`NEXT_PUBLIC_REAL_API_URL`). Viewports: 1024×768 (layout, sheets, long-value probes) and 1440×900 (everything else). Real Cognito sessions for all six roles (the Staff account's 職位 is 正社員スタッフ, not 店舗責任者; Manager/Staff/Observer see only the 5 members of S-003 JOYFIT 横浜).

**Corrections to the Known-gaps table found during the run:** オプション追加 (B5) and オプション変更 (B6) are **no longer on the mock** — they call the real `POST …/option-contracts` and `POST …/option-contracts/{id}/switch`. Those cases were run as real writes; their `Block (B5/B6)` treatment is obsolete.

**Bugs (grouped):** BUG-C-01 契約サマリー 月額合計 uses tax-excluded amounts and still counts 解約済み options (TC-141, 142); BUG-C-02 変更 disabled while a plan change is pending (TC-035); BUG-C-03 submit buttons fire twice on double-click — 変更申請を送信, 追加する, オプションを追加, 解約 (TC-037, 070, 103, 121); BUG-C-04 合意日時 in the future not validated (TC-028, 097, 117; Spec-only U4-e — the 60-day part was dropped 2026-10-01); BUG-C-05 事由 / 解約理由 have no 500-character cap (TC-063, 064, 115); BUG-C-06 調整後金額: ¥0 rejected, decimals blocked silently (TC-060, 062); BUG-C-07 withdrawn member keeps 調整を追加 and option 変更/解約 usable (TC-090); BUG-C-08 option 状態 vocabulary 適用中 vs 有効, and 解約済み options vanish from the list (TC-076, 119); BUG-C-09 no-contract member shows ¥0 placeholders in 契約サマリー (TC-010); BUG-C-10 sheet width 384 px vs ≈540 px (observed in TC-017, not filed as NG).

**Backend findings (Block (BE)):** adjustments whose period ended still come back `active` (TC-008, 041, 044, 048); repeated cancel of a cancelled option returns 200 instead of 409 E-OPT-006 (TC-122); 変更履歴 has no fee-adjustment source, and plan/option records omit destination/name/timing/reason (owned by Unit 10 TC-109 – TC-111).

**Run 2 (2026-09-30, unblock pass):** cases blocked by fixtures were re-run with fake data (Playwright route edits of real GET responses; app clock fixed for the date cases) and marked as such in Notes; audit / head-up cases were moved to Units 10 and 1 (where TC-109/110/111 and TC-041/131 already own them). New bugs: BUG-C-11 no 個別会費調整 適用中 badge on 主契約 (TC-008); BUG-C-12 no cancellation-fee-period block (TC-085, Spec-only); BUG-C-13 long plan / option / campaign names are neither truncated nor wrapped in the tables, pushing 月額 / 状態 / ⋯ out of view at 1024 (TC-161–163). TC-016 and TC-089 were removed (PO: spec = FE, the mismatch is BE-only and lives in the report). TC-143 was fixed in run 4 (BUG-C-14).

**Run 3 (2026-09-30, text / CSS fixes):** FE fixed in `contracts-tab` and re-verified in the browser — sheet widths 540 / 480 px (BUG-C-10), long plan / option / campaign names truncate with a tooltip (BUG-C-13), option active label 「有効」 (BUG-C-08, text part). TC-009 is OK (the fee comes from the member API), TC-017 / 161 / 162 / 163 are OK, TC-076 stays NG (解約済み never listed).

**Run 4 (2026-09-30, logic fixes):** all remaining NG were fixed in `contracts-tab` and re-verified in the browser: 月額合計 tax-included, without cancelled options and with the 休会費 while suspended (C-01); 変更 no longer blocked by a pending change (C-02); one request per submit via `useSingleFlight` on the plan, fee, add, change and cancel sheets (C-03); 合意日時 window on the four contract sheets (C-04); 500-character caps on 事由 and 解約理由 (C-05); ¥0 accepted and decimals/empty messaged (C-06); withdrawn members get no write controls (C-07); cancelled options listed as 解約済み (C-08); 「—」 total without a contract (C-09). TC-008 and TC-085 were withdrawn as tester mistakes — the page already reads the member-level fields I had not faked.

**Re-run 2026-10-05 (localhost:3000, same backend, 1440×900):** TC-029 rewritten for the dropped 60-day window → OK. The 主契約変更 sheet has no confirm step, so the run created plan-change application 9dee7c0a-6f51-4054-bc82-629d9f7a48d7 on F-PLAN (listed below).

**Re-run 2026-10-12 (localhost:3000, same backend, 1440×900, HQ):** option 状態 now maps every API `contractStatus` (MR review: `used` was shown as 有効 with 解約 enabled). New TC-167 → OK (fake data via route interception — the dev member has no `used` option); TC-076 / TC-084 re-checked → OK. No real write was made.

**Run 2026-10-12 (2) (localhost:3000, same backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, HQ, 1440×900):** option-contract pagination and server-side option search (MR review: only the first 100 contracts were read; the pickers filtered client-side). New TC-168 – TC-179 → 12 OK. Fake data via Playwright route override for TC-168/169/171 (60 contracts) and TC-175 (60-option master) and one switchable row for TC-177; the rest ran on real data (JF-0000008). No write request was sent. Observation (not NG): after an emptied last page the card steps back and shows that page's cached footer until it refetches (TC-171).

**Run 2026-10-12 (3) (localhost:3000, same backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, HQ, 1440×900):** option exclusion in オプション追加 / オプション変更 (MR review: the held options were taken from the paged table, so contracts outside the loaded page were missed). The pickers now read the member's live contracts on their own (`liveOnly=true&limit=100`, every page, only while a sheet is open) and stay locked until that read has finished. New TC-180 – TC-188 → 9 OK. TC-180 ran on real data (JF-0000008); TC-181 – TC-188 used Playwright route overrides of the option-contract and option-master reads (fake data, marked in Notes). TC-187 intercepted the POST, so **no write reached the backend**. **BUG-C-15** (found by TC-188 on the first pass, fixed in `contracts-tab`): a master page made only of held options left the picker on 「追加できるオプションはありません」 with the next page never requested; the next page is now requested while the visible list is empty; re-run → OK, TC-175 regression OK. Open for the PO: whether `used` and `pending_cancel` contracts should block re-adding an option (the server's `E-OPT-005` is not defined in the design; the screen follows `liveOnly` = `active` / `pending_start` / `pending_cancel`).

**Re-run 2026-10-15 (localhost:3000, same backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, HQ, 1440×900):** bug report 2026-10-14 — typing `test` into 調整後金額（税込） was accepted and the browser's English 「Please enter a number.」 bubble appeared (BUG-C-14). FE fixed in `fee-adjustment-sheet` (digits-only text input, full-width folded, form `noValidate`) and spec / parity / quickstart updated (tabs FR-014, US2 AC-4, PAR129). TC-062 rewritten and re-run → OK; new TC-189 (no native bubble) → OK; TC-054, 060, 061 re-checked → OK (notes appended). Nothing was created on the backend — every submit was blocked client-side.

**Reset 2026-10-29 (price basis):** the API now stores plan / option / 休会費 amounts tax-included (BE MR !880, API-085 v0.12 / API-087 v0.14) and the screen shows them as returned instead of adding tax. Cases whose expected values depend on that basis were reset to Untested: TC-006, 009, 017, 018, 020, 021, 075, 091, 092, 098, 099, 105, 106, 141, 142, 143, 165, 176.

**Run 2026-10-29 (price basis re-run):** 18 reset case(s) re-executed against the real dev API (`https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`) on branch fix/tax-included-price-masters, HQ account `test_hq_1`, 1440×900 (+1024×768 for layout): 18 OK, 0 NG, 0 Block (TC-009 / 143 / 106 / 165 with fake data via route interception; TC-098 / 099 real writes, both option contracts cancelled afterwards via UI 即時解約). Every amount is shown exactly as the API returns it (tax-included), with no tax added.

**Re-run 2026-10-30 (A-03 不具合 No4 — 合意日時 timezone):** app `http://localhost:3000`, backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, HQ pool account `test_hq_1`, 1440×900, browser timezone **Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh (UTC+7)** (TC-159 and TC-125 also on Asia/Tokyo). New TC-193 only — 主契約変更, オプション追加, オプション変更 (fake monthly option) and オプション解約 on JF-0000008, every write aborted at the browser (nothing created). Same defect as A-01-01-b TC-159 (the shared picker read the browser timezone); fixed 2026-10-30 and verified → OK.

**Run 2026-11-03 (localhost:3000, backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, HQ `test_hq_1`, 1440×900):** MR review comment (k.iwaki) — pasting `1200.0` into 調整後金額（税込） was silently stripped to `12000`. FE changed: typing stays digits-only, a pasted / dropped / autofilled value is kept as entered and validated at once (「調整後金額は半角数字のみで入力してください」); tabs FR-014 / US2 AC-4, quickstart, PAR129, test-plan updated. TC-062 and TC-189 rewritten and re-run; new TC-190 – TC-192 → 5 OK. Real clipboard paste in Chromium; every POST `…/fee-adjustments` was intercepted, so nothing was written (TC-192 step 5 skipped).

**2026-11-19 (ladyroom):** TC-194 – TC-217 added (Untested) — the `[Ladyroom]` slice (LR1–LR3); run in the 029 cross-feature order (Common preconditions, 「Ladyroom slice」).

**Run 2026-11-19 (ladyroom, local BE):** app `http://localhost:3000` (branch `feat/e02-area-master`, Next dev webpack) against the **local** fitness-backend-v2 BFF `http://localhost:4000` (`NEXT_PUBLIC_REAL_API_URL`) — dev has no area API (AREA-BE pre-flight: `GET /admin/areas?limit=1` → 200 locally). Sessions minted by magic link: HQ `admin@example.com` (all cases; its local 職位 Headquarters Admin lacked the オプション操作 flag, so `can_edit_member` / `can_create_proxy_application` were switched on for the run and off again after). Viewports 1440×900, 1024×768 for TC-216. AREA-MOCK: all three candidate lists and all three writes go to the real API (`memberGender` is sent and the server filters). Stores: plan cases on S-001 「JOYFIT24 渋谷 (dev)」 (QA 主契約 MC-0010 – MC-0013, MC-0018 – MC-0020 linked); option cases on S-003 「JOYFIT 横浜 (dev)」 — the JOYFIT QA options cannot be linked to the JOYFIT24 store (E-STR-011), so OP-0012 / 13 / 14 were linked to S-003 and switched to 有効 for the run only. Members: LR-F JF-9000002 / JF-9000023, LR-M JF-9000001 / JF-9000005, LR-P JF-9000024; LR-O / LR-U = JF-9000007 and JF-9000009 with 性別 set temporarily in the local DB (other → prefer_not_to_say → null, restored to male); LR-TF JF-0000001 / LR-TM JF-0000002 = throw-aways via `POST /admin/applications` (S-001, CP-0003 linked to S-001 for the call and unlinked after, approved). Result: 22 run → 22 OK (3 after FE fixes), TC-209 / TC-217 left Untested (deferred to 029 §9 step 13). **Bugs (all fixed 2026-11-19 in `contracts-tab`, re-verified in the browser):** BUG-C-16 主契約変更 with no gender-matching plan kept 「翌月月初…から適用されます。」 in the info alert and put the gender line under the select (TC-198, `main-contract-card.tsx`); BUG-C-17 オプション追加 / 変更 picker stayed enabled with 「選択してください」 when nothing was left to offer (TC-203, TC-208, `option-contracts-card.tsx`); BUG-C-18 long plan names hid 「¥n,nnn/月」 in the list and a 100-char token overflowed the オプション追加 summary box at 1024 (TC-216, both files). Observations (not NG): the screen trusts the server's `memberGender` filter and does not re-filter rows, so fake gated rows served against that filter are listed (TC-202 fake variant, TC-203 OPT-M-only variant, TC-204) — TC-202 was judged on its real alternative. Backend: a plan change for a member with an uncollected enrollment billing is refused 422 `E-MEM-205` with the message 「…精算後にアカウントを削除してください。」 (account-deletion wording on a plan change — BE).

**Step 13 run 2026-11-19 (ladyroom, local BE):** TC-209 and TC-217, run inside 029 §9 step 13 while E-02-G TC-063 held `QA-E02-AREA-F` at **男性のみ** (restored to 女性のみ afterwards). Same app / BE / HQ session; HQ 職位 Headquarters Admin got `can_edit_member`, `can_create_proxy_application`, `can_view_application` for the run (off again after) and OP-0012 / 13 / 14 were 有効 for the run (無効 again after). Result: 2 OK — TC-209: the three candidate lists follow the change after a reload (LR-M gains PLAN-F / -LJ / -LT / -NOBODY and OPT-F, LR-F loses PLAN-F / -LJ / -LT and OPT-F) and go back after the restore; a sheet left open, or closed and reopened without a reload, keeps the list it loaded (T16). TC-217 (observe only): LR-TF's booked change to PLAN-F and its 翌月適用 OPT-F still render with no notice; 「⋯」 on OPT-F offers 解約 only (変更 disabled) both while gated and after the restore. Observation (not graded): in the first attempt LR-M's open 主契約変更 sheet was gone when its tab was brought back to the front after the area was saved in a second tab of the same session (the member reads were refetched); not reproduced when the other tab only loaded a page. No write was made. Recount: 210 OK / 0 NG / 0 Untested / 0 Block.
